# Translation notes: 大学物理（第2版）

Every forced scenario of the translation of `College Physics 2e` into zh-CN
(`docs/prompts/translate.md` item 4), and anything else the owner should see: one entry per
place, with the file, the span or line, what was done and why.

| File | Span or line | What was done | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| book.json | `title` | 大学物理（第2版） | No official Chinese edition of OpenStax College Physics exists. 大学物理 is how Chinese names the university general-physics course and its textbooks; the edition is marked （第2版）, the Chinese convention, not `2e`. Owner to confirm; OpenStax University Physics would also render as 大学物理, so a future translation of it will need a distinguishing title. |
| book.json | `concepts` outside chapter 1 | kept in English | The concepts table is copied whole so every id matches the source; only the 34 concepts of sections 1.1–1.4 are translated in the pilot. |
| glossary.zh-CN.json | every row with `"confirm": "termonline"` | term taken from the named published authority | termonline.cn could not be reached from the build environment (DNS failure and proxy 403); each term is to be confirmed there and its entry URL added. |
| book.json | `types[stiffness].label` | 劲度系数 | The book's "force constant" for Hooke's k; 劲度系数 is the Chinese standard name of a spring's k, while 力常量 names the molecular force constant. |
| book.json | `types[lens-power].label` | 光焦度 | Collision (translate.md 4.5): power is 功率; the power of a lens takes the authority's distinct term. |
| glossary.zh-CN.json | English units / imperial system | 英制单位 / 英帝国单位制 | Collision (translate.md 4.5): 英制 alone would name both; the imperial system takes the qualified form. |
| book.json | `concepts[approximation].statement` | “粗估” for “guesstimates” | Forced scenario 4.2: an English blend word, rendered for meaning in the quotation marks the book gives it. |
| ch01 text.html | every figure eyebrow | `Figure N.M` and `Sim` kept | Forced scenario 4.6: the checker matches the eyebrow against its row exactly; the app localises it. |
| ch01 text.html | figure citations in prose | to be written 图 N.M by the section agents | Forced scenario 4.6: the app links only the English `Figure N.M`, so a translated citation stays plain text until the app's link pattern reads 图. |
| book.json | `exercise_kinds[].label` | 理解检测, 习题, 思考题, AP 备考练习 | The book's own labels have no authority; 思考题 (not the literal 概念题) is the established Chinese label for a conceptual question. Owner to confirm. |

## Chapter 1 sections

The forced scenarios the five section translators recorded (folded in from their `glossary-pending.json` files in the 2026-10-22 merge), grouped by section. The intro recorded none.

### ch01/1.1

| File | Span or line | What was done | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| ch01/1.1/figures.js | sim-atom-model | plural branches z === 1 ? ' electron' : ' electrons', ' proton'/' protons', ' electron goes'/' electrons go' and the readout unit '\text{ electron}'/'\text{ electrons}' collapsed to one Chinese form (个电子, 个质子) | English grammar in figure code (4.1) |
| ch01/1.1/figures.js | sim-atom-model | the a/an article chosen by /^[aeiou]/ collapsed to '' (const an = '') | English grammar in figure code (4.1) |
| ch01/1.1/figures.js | sim-atom-model | WORDS written 一, 两, 三 … (两 for two, as Chinese counts with a measure word); the slider label \text{electrons} written \text{电子数} | Chinese has no plural, so the bare plural noun that names a count becomes 电子数 (4.1) |
| ch01/1.1/text.html | classical-modern | The word physics comes from Greek: *物理学*（physics）一词来源于希腊语, the English word kept in parentheses | a sentence about the origin of an English word (4.3) |
| ch01/1.1/text.html | models | the Theory of Evolution and the Theory of Relativity written 进化理论和相对论这两种理论, the second theory span moved onto 这两种理论 | 相对论 already contains 论, so 相对论理论 would double it; the two theory spans and the relativity span are kept (4.5-like collision of the term with its own name) |
| ch01/1.1/text.html | realm, applications, models, classical-modern | within some paragraphs the order of data-concept spans differs from the source (e.g. 光速的约 1% inside data-type velocity, 经典物理学的定律, 物理学定律) | Chinese word order; every span is kept in its block with the same multiset |
| ch01/1.1/figures.js | sim-atom-model shell legend | operands n and SHELL[s] swapped to read 'SHELL 个电子中的 n 个' | Chinese word order |

## Chapter 2 sections

The forced scenarios the nine page translators recorded (folded in from their `glossary-pending.json` files in the 2026-10-22 merge), grouped by section, after the rows that hold for the whole chapter.

### ch02 (whole chapter)

| File | Span or line | What was done | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| ch02/2.5/section.json | exercises[p18].answer.solution, `v_{\text{max}}` | `\text{max}` and `\text{min}` kept as written where they are a subscript or label (a translator's `\text{最大}` put back to `\text{max}`); `\text{min}` meaning minutes is a unit and stays too | Chinese textbooks keep max and min as symbol abbreviations, as 1.3 keeps `\text{unc}`; translating one would change the symbol (translate.md 4.3) |
| ch02/2.8/section.json | exercises[cyu1].figure.caption | 'Figure 2.50' written 图 2.50, while the figure eyebrows `Figure N.M` and `Sim` in text.html stay English | an exercise figure caption is a citation of a figure in prose, which is translated (4.6); the eyebrow is chrome the checker matches exactly |
| every ch02 text.html, section.json and figures.js | "at various times", "time equal to zero", initial/final time, "chosen time" | a point in time written 时刻 (初时刻, 末时刻, 选定时刻); the quantity time and the interval stay 时间 and 时间间隔 | Chinese separates 时刻 (an instant) from 时间 (a duration) where English says time for both; the glossary rows instant and time carry the rule |
| every ch02 text.html | boxed-note eyebrows `Misconception Alert`, `Take-Home Experiment` | written 误区提醒 and 居家实验, the glossary rows (also in `建立联系：居家探究——…` and `误区提醒：…` headings) | labels of the book's own apparatus have no authority; they are rendered once and listed for the owner (translate.md 2.6) |

### ch02/2.4

| File | Span or line | What was done | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| ch02/2.4/text.html | average-acceleration, first paragraph | 'to accelerate means to speed up' rendered “加速”的意思是变快, and 'The accelerator in a car can in fact cause it to speed up' rendered 汽车的油门确实能使它加速 | The sentence is about the everyday meaning of the English word accelerate, whose root it shares with accelerator; 加速 alone would make the first sentence say that 加速 means 加速, so the gloss uses 变快 (4.2/4.3) |
| ch02/2.4/text.html | vector, sim-turning and cq1 solution: 'it is accelerating' of a car whose direction changes | rendered 具有加速度 (has acceleration), not 在加速 | Collision (4.5): the glossary fixes accelerate and speed up both as 加速, but the book here uses accelerating for any change of velocity, including a pure change of direction, where 在加速 would say the car speeds up |
| ch02/2.4/text.html | subway, 'the shuttle' | rendered 这列往返列车 for 'the shuttle' (a span of data-ref train) | The book calls the subway train a shuttle once; the Chinese keeps the word's sense (4.4, no equivalent in one word) |
| ch02/2.4/figures.js | accelSim subject 'the horse' / 'the train' | the articles built into the subject strings dropped: 这匹马 / 列车 (the subject is spliced into several story() sentences) | English articles have no Chinese form (4.1) |
| ch02/2.4/figures.js | accelSim story(), dirName and the due-west small line | 'to the ' + dirName(a), 'due west/east' and 'westward/eastward' rebuilt as 指向 + 东/西/右/左, 正西/正东 and 向西/向东 in the same branches | Direction phrases assembled from English prepositions and adverbs by code (4.1) |
| ch02/2.4/figures.js | topline / headline openings 'After t s …', 'At t s …', 'In t s …' | each opening that began with an English word before the number is written 经过 t s 后 / 在 t s 时 / 在 t s 内 with a leading string so the code order is kept | English prepositions put the number mid-sentence; Chinese needs a leading word to keep the same concatenation (4.1) |
| ch02/2.4/figures.js | sim-subway-velocity topline, 'P percent of the way along a trip of D km' | written 已完成百分之 P，这趟行程共 D km, the number order of the source kept | English word order 'percent of the way along a trip of' cannot keep the same two numbers in order in one Chinese phrase (4.1) |

'give' : 'gives')` after the rockets-burning phrase | the singular/plural verb branch removed; the sentence is built as WORD[n] + 各 + thrust + 对抗 + friction + 使 2,100 kg 的滑橇获得 + acceleration, with the display array WORD ('One rocket' … 'Four rockets') translated word for word as 一枚火箭 … 四枚火箭 | English grammar in figure code (translate.md 4.1): Chinese verbs do not agree with number |
| ch04/4.3/text.html, ch04/4.3/section.json, ch04/4.4/text.html, ch04/4.4/section.json, ch04/4.5/text.html | #mass-weight and its two notes (4.3), summary_html, cq6 solution and cq10 prompt (4.3), Figure 4.9 caption and ap7 prompt and hint (4.4), #normal-force first sentence (4.5) | weight (the force w = mg) stays 重力. Where the source explains it through 'gravity', 'the gravitational force' or 'the force of gravity', the explanans reads 引力: 重力是作用在物体上的引力的度量; 重力是引力，并且会随各地引力的不同而变化; 进入引力较强或较弱的区域; 这是因为月球上的引力较弱; 'defined as the force of gravity acting on an object of mass m' written as 引力. Where 'gravity' or 'the gravitational force' stands alone for the Earth's pull on a body it reads 重力 (the 4.4 Figure 4.9 caption's w, ap7's 539 N and its hint, the 4.3 cq10 basketball prompt and cq6's rock at the top of its flight, 4.7 p1, 4.8's 'our weight', which were already 重力 and stand). Where it is the interaction between bodies or the fundamental force (4.8's four basic forces, Earth on the Moon, 'gravity holds…', 'such as the gravitational pull', the law of gravity) it stays 引力. In 4.5 'Weight (also called force of gravity)' reads 重力（也称引力）. The everyday word for mass, as in 'mass and weight used interchangeably' and 'weight in kilograms', is 重量 (rows weight (everyday sense), losing weight 减重), and a load is 重物 (row weight (an object used as a load)). The heading rows with 重量 for the force became 重力 (质量与重力, 斜面上的重力, 把重力分解为分量) with old_target. | collision (translate.md 4.5): the authority's 重力 is the Chinese for weight and for the force of gravity, and 引力 for the gravitational force, so a literal rendering explains 重力 by 重力 (circular); the book also calls the Earth's pull on a body, its w, the gravitational force. The rows weight, gravity, force of gravity and gravitational force fix the split, the last one with the note that says where 重力 is meant |

## Chapter 8 sections

The forced scenarios the eight page translators recorded (folded in from their `glossary-pending.json` files in the 2026-10-22 merge), grouped by section. The lab and physics carts were one scenario in 8.3 (the glossary’s cart 手推车 against the carts of a track): the merge made them 小车 in the glossary rows too, and the pages already read so, so it is no longer a divergence and has no row. Neither is the launcher of 8.5 problem 7 (发射器, the row launcher) beside the launch vehicle 运载火箭 of its text, both printed by the source. The 8.5 strings of a racquet (拍弦 on the page) and the 8.3 belly flop (腹部拍水) were changed to 拍线 and 肚皮拍水 to agree with 8.1 and 8.2. The 8.1 problem 3(c) catapult, the racquet impact, the lunar crater and the pool-table bumper are rows of their own beside the existing catapult, impact, crater and bumper rows. 8.4, 8.5, 8.6 and 8.7 recorded none.

## Chapter 9 sections

The forced scenarios the seven page translators recorded (folded in from their `glossary-pending.json` files in the 2026-10-22 merge), grouped by section. The merge dropped one of the 9.2 rows: the subscripts of 9.2 problem 5, which the translator had written \text{顺时针} and \text{逆时针}, are back to \text{cw} and \text{ccw} as in 9.4, since an abbreviation used as a subscript stays as printed (as \text{ave}, \text{net} and \text{Eff}). The same decision put \text{net} back in every equation of the chapter (the translators had \text{合力} and \text{合力矩} inside the math), which is what turned the 9.2 sentence “where net means total” into a row of its own: the English word stays in the sentence beside the Chinese 合. Not rows: the headings the glossary fixes (三种平衡 became 平衡的三种类型, 为什么肌肉附着在靠近关节处 became 肌肉为什么附着在靠近关节处, and so on), which are the glossary’s rows and not divergences; the citation of the chapter 10 section in 9.6, which is now the glossary’s “有限大小物体的二维碰撞” in the book’s quotation marks, plain text because chapter 10 is not built when the page is read.

## Chapter 10 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter: rotational inertia and moment of inertia are both 转动惯量 (the book uses them as synonyms), and only the 10.3 heading “Rotational Inertia and Moment of Inertia” reads 转动惯性与转动惯量; pivot is 支点 everywhere; axle is 转轴 for the axis a body spins about (the gyroscope's and the bicycle wheel's in 10.7) and 车轴 only for a vehicle's axle in the 10.3 bicycle sim; linear kinetic energy is 平动动能 and linear motion 直线运动; rpm in prose is 转每分 (the symbol stays inside `\text{}` and in sim unit labels and `answer.unit`); the word prefix net in math is `\text{合力}\;F`, `\text{合力矩}\;\tau` and `\text{合外力的功}\;W` (10.5 and 10.6 had `\text{net}`, and the six forms and one statement of the chapter's rows were changed with them), while the subscripts `_{\text{net}}`, `_{\text{ball}}`, `_{\text{nail}}` and `_{\text{disk}}` stay English; the retarding torque of Earth's rotation is 阻碍力矩 (the page read 减速); example headings are `例题 N.M · 标题` throughout. The scenario rows below are those the page translators recorded that are forced; choices that the glossary or translate.md already decides (the \text{} words, rpm, weight, the pivot) are not listed.

## Chapter 11 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter: atmospheric pressure is 大气压 everywhere (the page translators wrote 气压 in about 90 places and the labels agent 大气压强 in the concept rows; 标准大气压 for standard atmospheric pressure, `\text{标准大气压 (atm)}` and 气压计 stay, and 气压 stands alone only in the 11.3 weather phrase 高气压和低气压天气系统); adhesion is 附着, adhesive force(s) 附着力, cohesion 内聚 and cohesive force 内聚力 (the 11.8 title, heading, chapter.json section title and the 11.9 citation read 液体中的内聚与附着), with the sentence in 11.9 now 靠液体的附着而贴在胸壁内侧; the figurative “ruler of pressures” of the 11.3 sim-poke and the “ruler of absolute pressure” of the 11.6 sim-head are 标尺 (the physical ruler stays 直尺); conversion factor is 换算因子 (11.3 hint and solution, the Table 11.2 caption row); cork as a material is 软木 (Table 11.1, the sim-ton, the 11.7 prose and hint) and the stopper stays 软木塞; the surface or waterline of a liquid in the 11.7 sims is 液面 (the sim labels and the ship sim-head); the example 11.9 heading reads 漂浮的女士; intraocular pressure is 眼内压 (the 11.4 problem read 眼内压强); a stranded submarine is 搁浅潜艇 (the pending row said 搁浅的潜艇, the pages 搁浅潜艇); the referent label of the 11.6 barometer tube follows the glossary, 气压计管; the 11.5 problem asking for “atmospheres” reads 标准大气压; the concept statements and the variable meaning that said 水银 read 汞. Weight follows the standing rule: 重力 for the force, 体重 for a person's weight in 11.9, 重量 for everyday weighing and for the 11.22 caption's submerged weight and weight in air, 称重 for the act. In 11.7's solution of the marbles problem the book's “direct push” and “extra push” read 推力 (the page had 压力); 压力 stays where the source says a fluid “presses harder”, a force. Of the 129 pending terms 113 became rows, 16 were not added (7 already in the glossary with the same target, 7 for atmospheric pressure whose rows changed instead, “net pressure”, which only the scaffold's source.md uses, and “Conversion Factors for Various Pressure Units”, the Table 11.2 caption row); 3 sense rows are new (ruler (scale of values), cork (material), the surface (of a liquid)) and 14 rows changed. The authority ids the translators gave to medical, mathematical and materials terms (cnterm-physics, cnterm-materials, cnterm-energy) were replaced by textbook, since the glossary has no such authority and none of those lists was consulted; every added row except the book's own labels carries `confirm: termonline`. The chapter's `forms` rows carry no `condition` (the source has none); the `\text{}` words of five form rows were translated (浸入的比例, 相对密度, 视重减小量, 视质量减小量, 排开的流体的重力/质量) in `latex` and `ktex` with `ost set`.

Owner item. **11.9 #eye, "A normal force here would be the weight of about 120 g".** The source's `text.html` wraps these words in `<span data-concept="normal-force">`, but "normal" here means typical (a normal intraocular pressure of about 15 mm Hg over 6.0 cm² gives about 1.2 N), not the force perpendicular to a surface. The span is a tagging error of the source edition. The source edition is never edited by a translation pass, so the span stays, and the translation writes 正常情况下的力 inside it (`（这里<span data-concept="normal-force">正常情况下的力</span>将相当于…`), which keeps the span count equal to the source and does not tell the Chinese reader 法向力. The owner may want to remove the span from the source line and from this edition together. The glossary target of `normal force` stays 法向力, so the collision will recur wherever the book says "normal" for "typical".

## Chapter 12 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter: nozzle is 喷嘴 everywhere in chapter 12 (the bare row nozzle, 喷口, was changed and the squid's nozzle of chapter 8 became the sense row nozzle (of a squid)); viscous drag is 黏性阻力 and the drag equation 阻力公式 (the concept viscous-drag, its name and terms, the F_V, F_s and v_t meanings and the viscous-drag figure labels followed); terminal velocity is 终极速度 and terminal speed 终极速率, swept through chapter 2 (2.7), chapter 5 (the concept terminal-velocity's `terms`, and 5.2's “（收尾）” now “（终极）”) and chapter 12 (the 12.4 notes, the concept sedimentation-rate, the v_t meaning, the 12.6 heading row); atmospheric pressure is 大气压 (12.3 and 12.5 had 大气压强 about twenty times, and the figure labels P_1 = atmospheric and P_2 = atmospheric) and standard atmospheric pressure 标准大气压; the 12.6 steel marble of the density slider is 钢弹珠 beside 冰弹珠, 铝弹珠 and 铅弹珠, as a sense row beside the 7.3 row steel marble 钢珠; the jet of water is 水柱 (the row jet of water), while the air jets of 12.2 stay 射流. The example headings keep the page form `例题 N.M · 标题`; the glossary's colon rows are lookup keys only. Where a harvested glossary row and a page disagreed and the sense was the same, the page was changed to the row: 12.4's 输液袋 became 输液瓶, 12.4's Example 12.8 heading now reads 这一流量, the referent labels and slider names of 12.1, 12.2 and 12.5 (插入水中的侧管, 气流挤过的管, 管 1，迎着流动的一端开口, 小块流体路径上的点 1/2, 点 1 处管的半径, 每根分支的半径, 沿血管的位置 (mm)) and the 12.2 figure phrase 高度保持不变. The remaining pending terms were folded as new rows; the rows front and back became front (of a sail) and back (of a sail), slab became slab (of fluid), start became start (of a random walk), and the phrase row the bar across the ball was dropped because the page reads “it”. The intro, 12.1 and 12.3 have no forced scenario. What the page translators listed as scenarios and the merge dropped as plain translation choices: the operand order of the 12.2 spd and hgt phrases and of the 12.3 sim toplines and parentheses, the 12.2 and 12.3 span-order sentences, the middle dot of the example headings, 大气压 against 大气压强, \text{常量}, the cited book title and `src` words, the 12.4 note that the Chinese names of sim-circulation need no code edit, nozzle 喷嘴 against 喷口, and the English `answer.parts[].part` words (owner item 1).

### ch12/12.2

| File | Span or line | What was done | Why |

## Chapter 13 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): the PV diagram is PV 图 with `<em>PV</em>` in HTML (it read P-V 图 in the rows); vapor is 蒸气 in general and water vapor 水蒸气; percent relative humidity is 相对湿度百分比; the feature label Things Great and Small is 大与小 and the 13.4 box eyebrow reads 建立联系：大与小：气体压强的原子与分子起源 (宏观与微观 dropped); atmospheric pressure is 大气压 (Table 13.2 row 大气压下); the absolute scale is 热力学温标 (not 热力学标度 or 热力学温度标度), the mercury thermometer 水银温度计, the equation of state 物态方程 and the mean square speed 方均速率 (the concept rows and the v2_bar and v_rms meanings follow; the description 速率平方的平均值 is gone); gasoline is 汽油 alone in the 13.2 dropdown; initial and final temperature are 初温 and 末温 as the glossary rows decide (the pages read 初始温度, 最终温度 and 末温度); new car smell is 新车气味. Of the 114 pending terms 20 were rows already in the glossary, 1 repeated another, 3 were dropped (initial pressure, calibration temperature and the plural atomic masses) and 90 are added, with Brownian motion beside Brownian effect; 11 glossary rows are changed. The page translators listed 63 forced scenarios; the 21 below remain. Dropped were the ones that left no difference in code and were plain word-order, span-placement, cited-title, unit-word or punctuation choices, and the ones the glossary or an owner decision now settles (大理石 for the stone, the 汽油（petrol） option, 大气压, 摩尔气体常量).

## Chapter 14 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): heat as a quantity is 热量 and heat transfer is 热传递 with the concept span on 热 only (`<span data-concept="heat">热</span>传递`; the pages had wrapped 热传递 whole in 14.3, 14.6, the intro and the 14.6 summary, and 热量传递 in 14.1, 14.2, 14.5, 14.7 and the intro, and 14.2 had 热量交换), 热传导 likewise `热` + `传导` where the page has both spans (14.5) and under the one span elsewhere; the calorie is 卡 (cal), the kilocalorie 千卡 (kcal) and the food Calorie 大卡 (the row Calorie 大卡 became 卡 and a sense row keeps 大卡 for the capital C of 2.2 and 15.1; 14.1’s sentence reads 食物卡路里（记作 Cal，即“大卡”）); temperature difference is 温差 (the pages, rows and the headings of 14.5 and 14.6 read 温度差); cork the material is 软木 (Table 14.3 and the sim option); vapor in general is 蒸气 and water vapor 水蒸气 (two bare “vapor” of the 14.3 dew-point solution read 水蒸气 and now read 蒸气); forced convection is 强制对流 (it read 强迫对流); “macroscopic movement of mass” is 物质的宏观运动 (the 14.6 summary read 质量的宏观运动); a person at rest is 休息时 (14.7 read 静止的人 and 静息速率); a circus rider is 骑手, a beam of radiation 辐射束, a net rate or net transfer 净, a block of material 物块, the Earth’s surface 地表 (14.4 and 14.7 read 地球表面), and rates of transfer are 速率 (7 places read 速度); example headings read `例题 N.M · 标题`; credits are as printed (14.7’s U.S. Army); cited titles are in “” (14.7 had 《》 for the chapters 24 and 29 and for two titles in its notes); the thermal insulator stays 隔热体 for this chapter. Where the glossary already had the English the glossary won over the pages: hot and cold body and object are 高温物体 and 低温物体 (the pages read 较热的物体 and 较冷的物体 in 14.1, 14.4 and 14.5; the concept statements and the Q_hot, Q_cold, T_hot and T_cold meanings follow), hot and cold face 高温面 and 低温面, heat exchange 热交换, pockets of air 气室 (14.5 read 气囊), the heater 取暖器 (the 14.6 house sim read 加热器), net flux of heat 热量的净流量, instant 时刻, cords 绳索, freeze-dried foods 冷冻干燥食品, the 14.1 heading 热量和功都能改变内能, the 14.5 heading 一天的热传导熔化一定质量的冰, 没有净热传导, 到目前为止传递的热量, 下落高度 and 木材（低端值，…）; the rows from the drink to the ice, from the ice to the drink and the example headings were changed to what the pages need instead. Of the 83 pending terms 7 were rows already in the glossary with the same target (thermal system, paddles, insulated can, good conductor, breathing passages, white hot, Electromagnetic Waves), 1 repeated a row of chapter 18 (free electron), 2 gave way to rows (pocket of air, heater), 2 repeated others (vapor (in general), mechanism (of heat transfer)) and 71 are added, 6 of them under sense-qualified sources (heat in heat transfer, Food calories, jar, Nitrogen, Oxygen, net); 5 sense rows are added for the decisions (forced convection, macroscopic movement of mass, at rest (a person), rate (of transfer), Calorie (food Calorie, capital C)), 76 rows in all, and 28 glossary rows are changed. The page translators listed 25 forced scenarios; the 12 below remain. Dropped were the ones that left no difference in code and were plain translation choices or kept the source as printed (the display words of SUBST translated in place, lower() on a Chinese name, the \text subscripts final, initial, hot, cold and warm, the wind-chill thermometer line break, 陆块 under the mass span, the name Gerardo García Moretti, the book’s own slips in 14.6, the order of spans in sentences, and answer.unit and answer.parts[].part kept English, owner item 1), and the ones the glossary or an owner decision now settles.

## Chapter 15 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): the PV diagram is PV 图 (`<em>PV</em>` in HTML where the source has the em; four concept rows read P-V 图 and were changed); the cylinder of an engine is 气缸 (new row cylinder (of an engine); the row cylinder of an engine changed from 汽缸, and 汽缸 was swept in 12.1, 15.3 and 15.7); the concept net-work keeps the chapter 7 name 合外力的功 while the phrases of the chapter (净功, 净输出功, 系统所做的净功) stand; heat as a quantity is 热量 and heat transfer is 热传递 with the concept span on 热 only (15.2, 15.5 and 15.6 wrapped 热传递 whole in 76 spans, now split; 15.1 read 热 for the quantity in “热和功” and “与热或功”, now 热量; 15.1’s caption read 热量传递和做功 and 15.3 had 热量 + 传递, now 热量和功 and 热传递); friction is 摩擦 for the phenomenon and 摩擦力 for the force, the translators’ usage kept; coin toss is 掷硬币 (one verb 掷 throughout); the vacuum chamber is 真空室 (15.3 read 真空腔室 and 腔室); the braking tag of the car in the 15.3 figure is 刹车 (it read 制动); the everyday mouse is 老鼠 (the 15.6 cq1 read 小鼠窝; the laboratory 小鼠 stays for the Haldane quotation); the dietary Calorie is 食物大卡 (it read 膳食大卡); 15.1’s one 大气压强 is 大气压; “in the 1800s” is 19 世纪; the PhET title in the 15.2 notes is kept in English (it read 物态); `\text{, 即 }` and `\text{即}` for “or” in math and `\text{比}` for “in” in 1 in 10^30 stand. Decisions of the chapter 14 merge applied here: temperature difference is 温差 (7 places in the text, 6 in the exercises and 5 concept rows read 温度差; the row Heat pumps and temperature difference already said 温差), the hotter and colder body are 高温物体 and 低温物体 (two places of 15.3), and the leftover 初始状态 of “initial state” is 初态. Of the 86 pending terms 10 were rows already in the glossary (the pages followed them: power station 发电厂 in the intro, the 15.7 heading 为什么无序比有序更可能出现, freezer 冷冻室 in 15.5), 8 repeated another pending term or were replaced (the loop of 15.3 and of 15.4, the leg of 15.4, the gauge of 15.6, the steam locomotive of 15.4, the splash image of 15.3, work out, cylinder holding a gas) and 68 are added with 3 sense rows (cylinder (of an engine), mouse (everyday), braking (of the car)): 71 rows; 6 glossary rows are changed. The page translators listed 26 forced scenarios; the 25 below remain. Dropped was the plain reorder of the spans coins, initial and final in the Example 15.9 question (a span-placement choice, every span stays in its block). The checker shows `figures.js: code` for 15.1, 15.2, 15.6 and 15.7 only; the comparison literal `'total'` of 15.6 sits inside a string and the checker does not read it.

## Chapter 16 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): oscillation is 振动 everywhere (mechanical; no 振荡 stands in the pages, the rows or chapter.json, and the one place that must tell vibration from oscillation keeps the English in parentheses, 16.2); friction is 摩擦 for the phenomenon and 摩擦力 for the force (note on the row friction, sense row friction (phenomenon)); a stiff system is 刚度大 (sense row stiff (system); the row stiff 僵直 stays for the stiff landing); crest of a wave is 波峰, crest of a hill 坡顶 (sense row crest (of a wave)); the suspension of a car is 悬架, the needle of a gauge 指针, the pulse of a wave 脉冲 (sense rows); the resultant wave is 合成波; the loops of a standing wave are counted as 波腹 (“n 个波腹”) and a loop as a stretch of the string reads 每一段 (both kept); cited section titles are in “” (16.5 read 《胡克定律：再论应力与应变》 in two places, now “…”); the introduction title is 振动与波导论 (it read 振动运动与波导论; the glossary row changed) with `short` 引言; sound is 声 in compounds and 声音 standing alone (16.1 read 热、声或动能, 16.9 read 乐器发出的声能取决于…, both now 声音; sense row sound (standing alone)); the Misconception Alert eyebrow stays 误区提醒 and the Engineering Application tag 工程应用. Glossary rows won over the pages: headlight 前照灯 (16.3 read 车头灯 in three places), compressions 密部 (the 16.9 solution read 压缩处 for the places where the coils bunch, now 密部), simple harmonic motion 简谐运动 (简谐振动 in 16.3, 16.6 and 16.10, now 简谐运动). The sliding block is 滑块 on every page (16.3 read 物块 in four lines of figures.js; sense row block (sliding), the row block being 街区). Of the 112 pending terms 13 were rows already in the glossary (the pages followed them), 99 became new rows (some merged: dissipative force of 16.1 and 16.5, the needle of 16.7, the crest of 16.2), and four rows were added by the merge (falling weight, equilibrium point, particle (point mass), stiff (system)). The translators’ sense rows and new terms are accepted (砝码, BB 枪, 班卓琴, 合回复力, 平衡位置 for the dashed-line label, 减速带, 千禧桥, 塔科马海峡大桥, 密部 and 疏部, 波源, 耗散力, 擒纵机构, 交流电路 with the span on 交流, 听力, 压强振幅, 声强, 波前); harmonic motion stays 谐运动 (the termonline cache lists no 谐振动 for it in 物理学名词, which gives 谐振子 and, in 力学名词, 简谐运动). `answer.unit` and `answer.parts[].part` stay English, `1.50 kHz`, 60.0 Hz electrical power as 电力 and `\text{rev/min}` stay as printed. The 16.3 readout `\text{at }` becomes `\text{在 }` with `\text{时}` appended, and the `\text{阻尼}` labels of the sliders (16.1, 16.7, 16.8) are plain translations of `\text{damping}`, not tabled. The page translators had listed 19 forced scenarios; the merge dropped 5 (the cited title of 16.1, two plain word-order choices without a code difference in 16.1 and 16.3, and the two of 16.11), merged two of 16.1 into one and added the 16.3 readout, which leaves 14 rows, tabled in `ch16/config.md` and below.

## Chapter 17 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): a shock wave is 冲击波 (the row changed from 激波; 17.7 read 激波 once, 17.4 already 冲击波); pitch of a sound is 音调 (new sense row pitch (of a sound), the row pitch now reads 音调 and pitch (of a vehicle) 俯仰 keeps the rotation of chapter 10); the book's “sound level” is its shorthand for sound intensity level, 声强级 (the row sound level changed from 声级), sound intensity 声强, decibel 分贝; hearing is 听觉 for the perception and 听力 for the ability (the translators' split kept, sense rows hearing protection and hearing damage added, hearing (ability) already stood from chapter 16); the ear canal is 外耳道 everywhere (the row changed from 耳道; 17.3, 17.5 and 17.6 and the concept rows read 耳道 in 14 places); the fundamental is the first harmonic and harmonics are numbered from it (the note of the row first harmonic corrected); tone is 音质 in Table 17.4, 纯音 for a pure tone and 声音 for “a 440 Hz tone” in the figure strings of 17.2 (sense row); aircraft and plane are 飞机 in this chapter (sense row aircraft (airplane) beside aircraft 航空器), the ultrasound probe is 探头 and a wave used as a probe 探针, the mouse of 17.4 problem 3 is 老鼠, and the title of 17.1 is 声音 (book row Sound). Where the glossary already had the English term the page gave way: V-shaped wake V 形尾迹, compressions 密部 in 17.4 (the pages read 压缩), observed frequency 观测频率 and received frequency 接收频率 where the term itself stands, age of the universe 宇宙年龄, recession of galaxies 星系退行, perception 知觉 in the 17.4 check-understanding answer, the five state labels of the 17.4 two-booms sim, 确定已知量 over 找出已知量 in 17.6, 无单位的量 over 无量纲量 (17.7 summary, the β_dB meaning), 音乐家 over 乐师 (17.4), 声振动 over 声音振动 (17.6), and 谐波次数 for the harmonic number n. The 17.3 derivation (decibel-difference) had been left in English (what, source, one cap and five src) and is translated. The concept timbre is named 音色 (it was 音质). The translators' scenarios were 32; the table below holds the 20 that are forced (the plain word-order choices, span placements that follow the glossary and the book's own slips kept as printed were dropped).

## Chapter 18 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): insulator is 绝缘体 and polarization is 极化 throughout (the general rows 隔热体 and 偏振 stay for their own chapters); the cloth of the rubbing experiments is 丝绸 (new row silk (cloth); the table row silk 蚕丝 keeps its note); electrically neutral is 电中性 (the bare 中性 of 18.3 and 18.7 and the figure label 中性原子 of 18.1 were changed); a counterpart particle is 对应粒子; household dust is 灰尘 (18.2 and 18.8 read 尘埃, which stays for interstellar dust); the eyebrow of the Things Great and Small box of 18.1 and its prose citation read 大与小：电荷的亚微观起源 (they read 大与小的事物：…); the test charge is 检验电荷 and the test object 检验物体 (18.4 read 试探物体; the two referent rows changed); the total electric field is 合电场 (18.7 and the chapter.json meaning of E_tot read 总电场; the two concept statements of 18.5 too); excess charge is 过剩电荷 (18.3 read 多余电荷), felt is 毡布 (18.8 read 毛毡), like charges are 同号电荷 (18.7 read 同种电荷), bleeding charge away is 泄放 (18.7 read 泄去 once), molten salt is 熔盐 (18.2 read 熔融的盐); Bloom levels are 布鲁姆层级 (18.1 read 难度层级, 18.3 Bloom 层级, 18.7 布鲁姆层次); the concept ink-jet-printer reads 喷头, 涂料 and 静电喷涂 as the page does (it read 喷嘴, 油漆 and 静电喷漆). Static equilibrium is 静电平衡 where the source means electrostatic equilibrium (18.7, 18.8) and 静态平衡 for 18.7 cq12. `ch18/config.md` holds 18 rows (18.1: 5, 18.4: 5, 18.5: 1, 18.6: 1, 18.7: 4, 18.8: 2) in one table; this file splits them by page. The page translators had listed 29; the merge dropped the plain word-order choices and the cited-title quotation marks, which are not forced, and moved the `answer.parts[].unit` question of 18.5 p3 to the owner list.

## Chapter 19 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): the plate separation is 极板间距 (the glossary row read 板间距; 19.2, 19.6 and the variable meaning of 19.2 changed, and the heading of Example 19.4 reads 两块极板之间…); “potential” for electric potential is 电势 everywhere, never a bare 势 (the sentence of 19.1 that says the English shortens the term keeps the English words, see 19.1); a battery’s or a generator’s terminals are 接线端 (19.5 read 两极, the exercise of 19.3 read 电极, the concept rows capacitor and zero-of-potential read 两个极), the signed ones 负极/正极 and the paddles of a defibrillator 电极板; the answers at the back of the book are 书后答案 (19.1 read 答案册, 19.3 答案表); the Bloom label is 布鲁姆层级 (19.3 read 布鲁姆层次, 19.6 难度层级); in 19.1 the force at the Earth’s surface is 重力 and the attraction between bodies 引力, the contours of the gravitational potential of a hill 重力势 and the potential of two masses 引力势; “at infinity” is 无穷远处 (new sense row; 无穷大 stays for the number) and the electrical ground is 地 (new sense row, 接地 for the verb); like and unlike charges are 同号电荷 and 异号电荷 (the concept rows read 同种/异种), the resultant field 合电场 (the concept superposition-of-potential read 总电场), sharp points 尖端 (the concept dielectric-breakdown-of-air read 尖点), living tissue 活组织 (19.1 read 生物组织), power lines 输电线 (the intro read 输电线路); the eyebrow of the Things Great and Small box of 19.5 reads 大与小：极化的亚微观起源, as that of chapter 18; the energy stored in a capacitor is the glossary concept row 电容器中储存的能量 in every title and citation; the cited title of 20.7 is “神经传导与心电图” (19.2 read 神经传导——心电图) and the chapter title cited in 19.5 is “原子物理学”; the headings of Examples 19.6 and 19.8 and the section heading of 19.3 follow the glossary rows (the pages read 会产生, 和 and 随), and six figure labels of 19.5 and 19.7 follow theirs. `ch19/config.md` holds 15 rows (19.1: 7, 19.3: 2, 19.4: 4, 19.6: 2) in one table; this file splits them by page. The page translators had listed 25; the merge dropped the plain word-order choices without a code difference (the 19.1 operand order of the Chinese clauses, the 19.2 toplines and readout, the 19.7 headline, the 19.6 span order), the 19.3 trailing phrases of two canvas labels, the 19.5 no-op `toLowerCase()`, and the terminals choice of 19.1, which is a glossary row.

## Chapter 26 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): the lens of the eye is 晶状体 and the optical element 透镜 (no page needed to change); the optical mirror is 面镜 (new sense row mirror (optical)) and the household mirror 镜子 (the shaving mirror of 26.2); upright is 正立 everywhere (26.1 and 26.4 read 直立 in the prose, the options and the alt text of the microscope problems, and the heading “Upright images” of 26.5 and its hover name, referent label and concept name changed from 直立); specimen is 标本, so the chapter-wide rows that said 样品 changed (four figure-label rows, the concept statements of numerical-aperture, immersion-objectives and microscopes-beyond-visible-light, and the variable meanings of n and θ in 26.4), while 样品 stays for the book's “sample” in 26.4; credits are as printed, so the Navy photo lines of 26.2 and 26.4, Oak Ridge National Laboratory and the Lewis Lab credit of 26.4 are English again; the glossary wins over the page translator for glass ceramic (微晶玻璃, the page read 玻璃陶瓷), Narrabri (纳拉布里, the page read 纳拉布赖), the focus names of 26.6 (红光的焦点，700 nm and 紫光的焦点，400 nm, the page read 红光（700 nm）的焦点) and the term mismatch in refractive indices (折射率失配, the page read 折射率的失配). The dishes of the Square Kilometre Array are 射电天线 (the alt text of Figure 26.27 read 碟形天线). The apparatus words are 书后答案 (the notes read 标准答案 and 给定答案, and 答案来自本书自己的答案) and 容差; the intro title is 导论 with the short 引言. LASIK is spelled out once (26.2) and bare elsewhere. `ch26/config.md` holds 10 rows (26.1: 1, 26.2: 1, 26.3: 2, 26.4: 2, 26.5: 2, 26.6: 1, intro: 1) in one table; this file splits them by page. The page translators had listed 17; the merge dropped the answer.parts[].part labels of 26.1 (a standing decision), the quotation marks round “yes” of 26.4, the word order of the figures of 26.4, the symbol mark α max, and the kept misprint of the 26.4 microscope problem, which is not a deviation and goes to the owner list, and merged the two 26.2 rows and the last two 26.3 rows. The Lewis Lab credit and the other credits are not scenarios.

## Chapter 28 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter: the relativistic factor γ is 相对论因子 everywhere (the glossary row read 洛伦兹因子; the source never says Lorentz; 28.2’s sim-twins aria was the one page string); the source of light is 光源 (the harvested rows source wavelength, source frequency and the variable-meaning and referent rows read 波源, which the pages and the concept rows never used); elapsed time is 时间间隔 (28.2 prompt and solution, the concept rows relativity-of-simultaneity, proper-time and observers-agree-on-relative-speed, and the meanings of Δt_0 and Δt read 经过的时间 and 经过时间); the cited 28.6 title is “相对论能量” (28.3 read 相对论性能量 in the text, the note and the exercise_notes); a solution step’s “equation” is 方程 (28.4 and 28.6 read 公式 in the ‘Choose the appropriate equation’ and ‘Plug the knowns into the equation’ steps; 公式 stays in the named formulas); the speed in the 28.6 aria is a fraction, 分数, as in every other page (it read 倍数); the gravity of GPS is 引力 (the intro read 重力); the Stanford Linear Accelerator carries the glossary 中心 (斯坦福直线加速器中心); Library of Congress stays as printed in the credit. Sense rows added: ship (spaceship) 飞船, platform (railway) 站台, speed limit (massive objects) 极限速率, probe (exploratory) 探测器, barrel (of oil) 桶, accelerator (particle accelerator) 加速器, TV (teravolt), gravity (of relativity and GPS) 引力. The 28.6 variable m is marked `redefines`. The page translators had listed 20 scenarios; the merge kept 10 rows (28.2: 3, 28.3: 3, 28.4: 1, 28.6: 3) and dropped the ones that are plain choices or the book’s own slips (the elapsed-time and relativistic-factor remark of 28.2 is resolved by the decisions above; the left/right subscripts, the millions of 28.3, the toplines of 28.3 and the elliptical ‘At 0.500c’ of 28.4 leave no difference in code; the broken sentences and the double full stop are the book’s and are listed in the owner items). Chapter 28 has no scenario in 28.1, 28.5 or the intro.

## Chapter 22 sections

Merge of 2026-10-22. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): a magnetic field line is 磁感线 everywhere and never 场线 (22.3, 22.4 and 22.5 read 场线 or the span 场 + 线 for it, 22.10 and 22.11 read 场线 and 磁场线; where the source span is on “field” alone it wraps 磁感, and the concept rows and variable meanings follow); the magnetic force is 磁力 (no page or row read 磁场力); a cosmic ray is 宇宙线 (22.5 read 宇宙射线 in the text, the figure strings and the exercises); the compass needle is 指南针的指针 (the pages, the concept rows electromagnet, direction-of-field-lines and torque-varies-with-angle and the variable meaning of B in 22.3 read 罗盘指针; the glossary row compass needle changes); the small compass that tests a field is 用来探测的指南针 (22.3 read 探针; the row probe stays 探针 for a physical probe, and the solution of 22.3 cq2 keeps 探针 because it speaks of the test charge as well); rms is 方均根 (22.10 read 均方根); the supersonic aircraft is 超声速飞机 (22.4 read 超音速飞机, 22.6 超声速航空器); the counter-torque of a meter is 反向力矩 (22.8 read 反力矩; the glossary row changes); a spiral path is 螺旋路径 (the glossary row spiral paths changes from 螺旋轨迹); the force per unit length is 单位长度上的力 (the hint of 22.11 p14, the variable meaning of l in 22.10, the concept force-between-parallel-currents and the name of force-per-unit-length-on-a-wire read 单位长度所受的力), and the turns per unit length are 单位长度上的匝数 (the concepts field-inside-a-solenoid and solenoid and the variable meaning of n in 22.9 read 单位长度的匝数 and 圈数); the current loop is 载流线圈 (22.2 read 闭合电流环路 and 22.3 圆形电流环); the magnetic field strength of the 22.4 exercise on the Earth’s surface is 磁感应强度 (the page read 磁场强度); in prose magnetism is 磁性 and 磁学 stays for the chapter title and the two cited section titles; the chapter introduction cited in 22.5 is “磁学导论” (the page read “磁学简介”); contrast is the glossary 衬度 (22.11 read 对比度); the Check Your Understanding box is “理解检测”框 in the notes of 22.2 and 22.10; the apparatus words are 书后答案 (22.4, 22.7 and 22.8 read 书中的答案表, 22.2 附有书中的答案), 建议思路 (22.2, 22.4, 22.7 and 22.11 read 建议解法) and 没有给出答案 (22.4, 22.7 and 22.8 read 没有标准答案); the starter is 起动机 (22.7 and 22.11 read 起动器); the PhET simulation of 22.11 is named “磁体与指南针”（Magnet and Compass） as 22.2 names “磁体与电磁铁”（Magnets and Electromagnets）, and the PhET word is 模拟 (22.11 read 仿真); the intro caption is 北极光 alone, the two English names dropped. The glossary already held nuclear radiation, Dees, MHD drive, the number of turns in the coil and inside diameter, and the sense row the wire (current-carrying) 导线 beside the 4.5 钢丝 row; the pages read those rows. `ch22/config.md` holds 27 rows (intro: 2, 22.1: 2, 22.2: 6, 22.3: 4, 22.4: 2, 22.5: 3, 22.8: 1, 22.10: 5, 22.11: 2) in one table; this file splits them by page. The page translators had listed 35; the merge dropped the ones that were plain choices (the glossary terms 铁磁性的 and 电磁学 in two definitions of 22.2 and the book’s literal phrase 产生电流的亚原子磁性, the position of the sentence words in the 22.3 and 22.2 toplines whose code is unchanged, the quoted PhET names) and merged duplicates (the three plural rows of 22.1 sim-split-magnet, the hover-name rows of 22.3, the weight and pinch rows of 22.10), and added the rows for the span wraps 磁感 (22.3, 22.4, 22.5) and for the intro caption. The 22.8 summary full stop stays recorded.

## Chapter 24 sections

The merge of chapter 24 (2026-10-23) folded the five `glossary-pending.json` files: 44 rows are new, 3 glossary rows change (Table 24.1's cell, and the two γ 射线 observatories, whose target lacked the space before γ), and the pending terms the glossary already had were resolved in its favour (source of a field 场源, EM wave 电磁波 in 24.2, swing as a playground swing kept apart from the FM swing 频偏, refraction, crest (of a wave), static (radio) 静电干扰, the loop of wire the magnetic field passes through 金属丝圈, static charge, pulsed laser, nuclear fusion, the HD format, the introduction title 电磁波导论). Sense rows separate the electrical senses from the mechanical ones: oscillation (electrical) 振荡 and oscillating charge/current 振荡电荷、振荡电流 beside 振动; source (of a field) 场源 and source (of a wave) 波源; cycle (of a wave) 周期 and the unit word 周 of cycle/s beside the thermodynamic 循环; electrical force in the book's list of four basic forces 电磁力. Receiver is 接收器 throughout (无线电接收机 stays where the book says radio receiver) and transmitter 发射器; rms 方均根; γ 射线 and X 射线 carry a space. The page translators' list had 22 forced scenarios; the merge kept 19 and dropped the plain word-order choices (span reorders, zero pronouns, 倍数 and 直角 phrasing, the oven topline).

## Chapter 23 sections

Merge of 2026-10-23. Decisions taken for the chapter (coordinator-directed): rms is 方均根 (23.12 read 均方根 in the text, the figure strings and the exercises); an appliance is 用电器 (23.8 read 电器 83 times, with 双重绝缘用电器 for the doubly insulated appliance); the normal to a surface is 法线 (23.5 read 垂线 in the text and the figure hover name, and the concepts magnetic-flux, flux-and-orientation and electric-generator and the θ meanings of 23.1, 23.2, 23.3 and 23.5 read 垂线 too; the glossary rows the perpendicular to the area and the perpendicular to the coil change); the final value is 终值 and the final current 终值电流 (23.10 read 末值 nine times and 最终值 and 最终电流 in the concepts rl-circuit, rl-time-constant and current-growth-in-an-rl-circuit; the glossary row final value stays 末值 for chapters 2, 7 and 13 and 终值 is a sense row); electrical safety is 电气安全 (23.7 read 用电安全, in the cited title too); the camera flash is 闪光灯 (23.9 read 相机闪光灯); the needle of the galvanometer is 指针 (23.1 read 针 fifteen times); the sorting ramp of 23.4 is 斜面 (the figure strings, the referent and the concept applications-of-magnetic-damping read 斜坡 in places, and four glossary rows change) while the ramp of a graph in 23.2 stays 斜坡; oscillation is 振荡 for charges, fields and circuits and 振动 for a mechanical body (23.5 read 振动的频率 for the emf, the concept applications-of-magnetic-damping read 振荡 for the balance); the field strength B is 磁感应强度 (the meanings of B in 23.1 and 23.4 and the concept magnetic-flux read 磁场强度, which is H in Chinese), while the qualitative “field strength” of 23.7 stays 磁场的强度; a bare magnet in a concept statement is 磁体 (the statements read 磁铁; 条形磁铁 stays); effective resistance is the glossary 等效电阻 (23.11 read 有效电阻); the radio tuner is the glossary 收音机调谐器 (read 收音机的调谐器) and the exact exponential treatment 精确的指数处理 (23.10 p9 read 精确的指数方法); the control “what moves” is 运动的是 in 23.1 and 23.3 (23.3 read 运动部分); the credit clause “modification of work by X” is 改编自 X 的作品 (the row changes from X 作品的改编). The apparatus words of the notes and exercise_notes follow the owner’s list: 布鲁姆层级 (23.1 read Bloom 层级, 23.12 布卢姆层级), 判分选择题 and 判分答案 (read 评分选择题 and 评分答案), 建议思路 (read 参考思路 and 建议解法), 容差 (read 误差), 习题卡片 (read 答题卡) and AP 题 (read 备考题, AP 备考题, AP 考试备考题 and, for the item, AP 备考练习). Part labels and figure citations in prose are full-width: 图 23.4（a）, 图 23.42（a）（b）（c） and 图 23.10（b）, and the solution strings of 23.1 ap1 and 23.9 p3, p4 and p6 read （c） and （a）（b）（c）（d）. Unit symbols in axis labels are written in half-width parentheses, t (s) and Φ (mWb) (24 strings in figures.js; the glossary rows follow); the canvas text of 23.9 keeps emf = in English. The page translators raised 148 pending terms (146 distinct sources) and 32 forced scenarios. 17 terms were glossary rows already or duplicates and are folded without a new row (appliance, camera flash, effective resistance, rms, period, earth/ground wire, earth/ground (the connection), cell phone, loops, pan, script E, Joseph Henry, Michael Faraday, the second what moves, test-prep item, and the two perpendicular rows, which change); 131 became rows, with two sense rows added by this merge (final value (of a current), ground wire (three-wire system)). The scenarios were cut to 26 rows: the translators’ answer.parts[].part and .unit rows (23.12, 23.7) are owner items, the cited title in “”, the dropped articles of 23.7 and 23.8 and the full-width solution letters are plain choices, the two react rows and the two operand-order rows of 23.3 are merged, and two rows were added for the template literals of 23.1 that the checker lists. The checker lists ten code replacements, all recorded below, and three untranslated? lines of pure math.

## Chapter 25 sections

Merge of 2026-10-23. Decisions taken for the chapter (orchestrator-directed; the glossary wins over page drift): the optical mirror is 面镜 (the sense row mirror (optical) already stands, so the pending rows mirror and mirror (optical component) are folded into it) and the household mirror 镜子; ray tracing is 光线追迹 (25.6 read 光线作图法, 光线作图 and 光线作图规则, now 光线追迹 and 光线追迹规则; the verb is 追迹, and one 追踪 stays in the definition “determining or following (tracing)”); camera is 照相机 (25.4, 25.6 and the figure code read 相机); the screen an image falls on is 屏 (25.7 read 屏幕; the intro keeps 屏幕 for the display, and the new sense row screen (optical) carries 屏); the gem is 钻石 and the material 金刚石 (new sense row diamond (gem)); the perpendicular is 法线 (25.5 read 垂线); total internal reflection is 全反射 (25.4 read 全内反射); speed is 速率 (25.3 read 速度 for the change of speed); Western Australia is 西澳大利亚州 and England 英格兰 (the glossary rows; the pages read 西澳大利亚 and 英国); contact lenses are 角膜接触镜 (the glossary contact lens; 25.6 read 隐形眼镜). Where termonline.cn was reachable (17 new rows), its form replaced the translator's: the laser pencil is 激光指示器 (was 激光笔), microsurgery 显微外科 (was 显微手术), the light pipe 导光管 (was 光导管), the concentrating collector 聚光型集热器 (was 聚光集热器). Apparatus words of the exercise notes are normalised to the book's labels (概念标签, 书后答案, 判分选择题, 建议思路, 习题卡片, 理解检测框; “没有给出答案” for unkeyed), the part labels and figure citations in prose are full-width （a）（b）, the cited title in 25.4 is in “”, and the three Flickr credits of 25.6 read as printed (a comma, not a slash). The page translators listed 21 scenarios; the merge dropped 6 that are plain translation choices (the fish-tank side word, the light-ray span and pronoun of 25.3, the articles inside ref spans, the span order, the “Image length” slip and the credits of 25.7) and added 2 (the colour predicate of 25.5 and “times as tall” of 25.6), so 17 remain. The checker shows a `figures.js: code` line for 25.2 (row 2 of 25.2).

## Chapter 29 sections

Merge of 2026-10-23. Decisions taken for the chapter: the introduction is titled 量子物理学导论 on the page, in the glossary row and in 14.7’s citation (the earlier row read 引论); modern physics is 近代物理学 (the introduction read 现代物理学); threshold frequency stays 截止频率 and the wavelength counterpart is 截止波长 (物理学名词 lists 阈值频率 and cutoff wavelength 截止波长; 红限 is the old name); the work function is 逸出功 beside the binding energy 结合能; the stopping voltage is 遏止电压; uncertainty is 不确定度 and the principle 海森伯不确定性原理; the solar cell is 太阳电池; the source of light is 光源 and of particles 粒子源 (sense rows); the book’s space sail stays 太空帆 while a solar sail is 太阳帆; headlights are 前照灯 (29.4 read 车头灯); the heat lamp is 加热灯 (29.3 read 取暖灯); the photographic plate is 感光板 (29.4 and 29.6 read 照相底片), the CCD camera 照相机 (29.4 read 相机); the rung of the oscillator ladder is 梯级 as the glossary row rung (29.1 read 横档/档); planes of atoms are 原子面 (29.6 read 原子平面); Louis-Victor is 维克多 (29.6 read 维克托); sterilization by weak UV is 灭菌 beside the glossary sterilize 消毒 (sterilizer); bremsstrahlung stays 轫致辐射 with 制动辐射 as its German gloss braking radiation; decay energy is 衰变能量 (the row read 衰变能); the unused candidate rows matter wave and wave function are dropped. UV and IR stay as printed where the source abbreviates them (29.3, 29.2’s UV photon) and the words are 紫外线, 红外线 and 紫外辐射, 红外辐射 where the source spells them. The photo credits are printed as in the source with （来源：…）; the two credits of 29.6 had a full-width comma and now read as printed. The exercise notes of every page use the book’s apparatus labels (判分选择题, 书后答案, 建议思路, 布鲁姆层级, 概念标签, 容差), the part labels in prose and in solutions are full-width （a）（b）, and the next section cited in 29.2 is in “”. The part labels of `answer.parts[].part` and the unit photons/s stay English (owner item 1); “threshold energy” in the Figure 29.7 + 29.8 caption is the book’s slip for the threshold frequency and is kept as printed.

## Chapter 27 sections

Merge of 2026-10-23. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed): the title of 27.1 is 光的波动特性：干涉 and its short 光的波动特性 (the glossary and chapter.json rows changed from 波动性; the prose wave nature of light stays 光的波动性); the path difference is 波程差 in 27.3 to 27.5 (the concept single-slit-minima read 路径差; 长度之差 stays for the variable Δl, “the difference in length”); the order is 级次; bright and dark fringes are 明条纹 and 暗条纹 (the row bright fringe and the concept double-slit-constructive read 亮条纹; the bands of 27.7 are read as fringes) and bright and dark lines 亮线 and 暗线; polarization by reflection and by scattering are 反射起偏 and 散射起偏 (the headings and example title of 27.8 already read so; the two concept names and three glossary rows changed from 反射偏振, 散射偏振, 计算反射偏振); the phase change on reflection is 半波损失 in the concept name and 相位变化 in the prose (the concept statements of phase-change-on-reflection and nonreflective-coating read 相位突变); the mirror of an optical system is 面镜 (27.2 read 镜子 three times for the apparatus mirror; the concept statement of diffraction-limits-resolution and the variable meaning of D_ap read 反射镜) and the household mirror stays 镜子; the specimen is 标本 and the sample 样品 (27.6 read 样品 for the specimen in the prose, the referent label, three figure labels and the variable meaning of d, and 27.9 in three places and in the confocal summary and statement); the shift of the wave in the 27.3 figure is 位移 as the glossary row shift and 27.7 (the page read 移动量); out of phase is 不同相 as the glossary (27.9 read 相位不同); the word “for” of an enumeration is `\text{对于}` (27.3 and 27.4 read `\text{对}`); the two-photograph credit of 27.4 is as printed in English (“via Flickr” was 经由 Flickr); the figure citations and the part labels of the solutions are full-width （a）（b） (图 27.3(b) and the like); the apparatus words of the notes follow the chapter decisions: 书后答案 (the notes read 标准答案 and 本书自己的答案), 判分选择题 (six wordings), 布鲁姆层级 (布卢姆), 建议思路 (建议解法, 参考思路), 习题卡片 (题卡), 容差 (误差), 说明 (备注, 注记) and 概念标签 (概念标注). The pending term shift (移动量) was not added, and the pending templates of 27.9 with … in them are figure strings built from numbers, not terms. `ch27/config.md` holds 9 rows (27.1: 1, 27.2: 1, 27.4: 1, 27.6: 4, 27.7: 1, 27.8: 1) in one table; this file splits them by page. The page translators had listed 15; the merge dropped the word-order choices of 27.1 and 27.2, the cited titles of 27.6, the `answer.parts[].part` labels of 27.6 (a standing decision) and the `src` of the 27.6 derivation (translated, not a deviation), and merged the two 27.4 rows and the two 27.6 million rows.

Other entries for this section:

- 23.3 exercises[cq2].prompt: “Why might the cylinder get live/hot” is the book's find-and-replace artefact for “hot” (the AI solution explains heating only). Kept as printed, 带电/发热, and recorded as a source slip.
- 23.12 exercises[p16], footnote c-p16-quarter: the book's key takes Δt = 1/100 s as the quarter cycle; the footnote and notes now say the key divides by 100 (the quarter cycle is 1/1600 s).
- PhET simulation names (23.2, 23.7, 23.12): one policy, PhET 模拟 followed by the translated name in “”; the links themselves stay omitted.
- Apparatus labels unified chapter-wide: 书后答案, “理解检测”框, PhET 模拟.

## Chapter 34 sections

Merge of 2026-10-23. Decisions taken for the chapter (coordinator-directed): gravity in the sense of the fundamental force or of general relativity is 引力 (Newton’s gravity that pulls the apple, which explains the Moon, read 重力 in 34.1 and now reads 引力; 重力加速度 stays for the acceleration of free fall); the 34.1 glossary rows win over the page for spontaneous symmetry breaking (对称性自发破缺; 34.1 read 自发对称性破缺 in the text, the sim and the summary) and steady state (稳恒态; the page read 稳态); the cosmic background radiation is 宇宙背景辐射 (sense row background radiation (cosmic), beside 本底辐射 of the laboratory; the exercise note read 背景辐射光子), the CMBR itself stays 宇宙微波背景; the Big Crunch is 大坍缩 and the flat universe 平直宇宙; the rotation curve is 自转曲线 with 自转速度 and 星系自转问题 (34.4 read 旋转…, in the text, the sim strings and a referent label); a size across is 线度 and a dimension of superspace 维度 (the variable D read 横向尺度 and now follows the row, 物体的线度); infancy is 起步阶段 in both 34.2 and 34.3 (34.2 read 初生阶段); “quantization of magnetic flux” is 磁通量子化 (34.7 read 磁通量量子化, the span now on 磁通); “galaxy formation” is 星系形成 (34.7 read 星系的形成). Mly and Gly stay units in math and answers, and a value in prose is written in 万/亿 (100 Mly is 1 亿光年, “less than 1 Gly” is 不到 10 亿光年, “10 Gly” is 约 100 亿光年, 2 Mly is 200 万光年). The apparatus words follow the owner’s list: 书后答案 (34.2, 34.3 and 34.6 read 本书自带的答案, 本书自己的答案 and 书中自己的答案), 布鲁姆层级 (34.6 read Bloom 层级), 建议思路 (34.5 and 34.7 read 建议的解答思路 and 建议解法), the no-answer wording of 34.7 (没有标准答案), and 行内题 for the inline questions (34.2 read 内嵌题 and 34.4 内联题). Cited section titles are in “” (34.2 read 《物理学前沿导论》 for the chapter opening and 34.7 《暗物质与闭合》); the part label in a hint is full-width (34.3 read (a)); the credit of the opening photograph is as printed, （来源：X-ray: NASA/CXC/CfA/R. Kraft et al.）. Of the 43 pending terms, 5 were rows already and the glossary won (line of sight, time travel, solar eclipse, spontaneous symmetry breaking, steady state), 1 repeated another (phase transition in 34.7) and 1 is built from rows (one-dimensional vibrating strings); the other 36 are added, and 2 rows the merge raised (background radiation (cosmic), WIMP) make 38 added rows; 21 rows are changed (the notes of 8 decision rows and 13 sentence rows that now follow the page or chapter.json). Seven harvested rows no page uses are dropped (Meissner effect, Cooper pair, BCS theory, superfluid, Planck length, billion light years, of its present size). The proton hover suffix 逃逸, the two idiom rows, 皱褶 and the MACHO and WIMP first-use spellings stay as the pages have them. The page translators listed 13 scenarios and 13 remain; the Mly and Gly row is rewritten for the 万/亿 decision and the Mly gloss row now reads 约 50 Mly（5000 万光年）. termonline.cn answered from this sandbox: 13 of the 38 added rows are confirmed with their entry URL; the others are plain words or compounds that termonline does not list and carry the provisional note.

## Chapter 33 sections

The merge of chapter 33 (introduction and 33.1 to 33.6) folded the page translators' 47 pending terms (45 distinct) into 42 new glossary rows and 15 corrections of existing rows (`glossary-new.json`). Four of the pending terms were rows already (Van de Graaff generator, electron family number, antibottom quark, and weak interaction, which the chapter 34 merge added first). The chapter's own decisions: the accelerator is a 装置 (row machine (accelerator)), the carrier is the 媒介粒子 and the range of a force its 力程 (a particle's range in matter stays 射程), gravity as one of the four forces is 引力, the weak force is 弱力 and 弱核力 where the source says force and 弱相互作用 only where it says interaction, and a counted family is a generation, 代, while a named family is 族 (电子族, μ 子族, τ 子族); the family rows were reconciled to say so. Page words replaced: the title of 33.3 (加速器由能量创造物质), 作用范围无限 to 力程无限 (33.6), the two gluon hover names of 33.6 (“where” is 在此处), 基本的构成单元 to 基本组成单元 (33.5) and 夸克构件 to 夸克组成单元 (33.4), 衰变能 to 衰变能量 (33.4), 静止的靶 to 静止靶 (33.3), 整数的内禀自旋 to 整数内禀自旋 (33.2), 粒子物理学的标准模型 to 粒子物理标准模型 and a proton-antiproton pair to 质子-反质子对 (33.6); the apparatus words of the notes and exercise_notes (布鲁姆层级, 概念标签, 书后答案, 建议思路, 习题卡片, 理解检测框, AP 题); cited titles and chapter names that stood bare (33.2, 33.6) now in “”; the part labels of figure citations and exercise prompts and solutions (33.5, 33.6) full-width （a）（b）. The page translators had listed 19 scenarios; the merge dropped the plain choices (the range 射程 of 33.4, the generation/family wording of 33.4, the part labels of 33.4 answer.parts, the 其对应的物质原子 of cq1) and merged the two hadron-name rows of 33.5, leaving 14. The checker shows `figures.js: code` lines for 33.1, 33.2, 33.3 and 33.5 only, all recorded here, and `untranslated?` lines only for pure-math answers.

## Chapter 30 sections

Merge of 2026-10-23. Decisions taken for the chapter (orchestrator-directed; the glossary wins over page drift): Bohr is 玻尔; the charge-to-mass ratio is 比荷 throughout (30.1 read 荷质比 three times); substructure is 子结构 (the intro, 30.1 and 30.7 read 亚结构, 30.5, 30.8 and 30.9 read 内部结构; the “underlying structure” of 30.1 stays 内部结构); series is 线系 and the three series 莱曼系, 巴耳末系 and 帕邢系; the film of the 30.3 grating figure is 底片 (new sense row) while the film of the 30.5 hologram is 胶片 and 照相胶片 (the figure code read 底片 and 感光底片; the negative, 实际上是底片, stays); stimulated emission is 受激辐射; the reference beam is 参考光 and the object beam 物光 (the 30.5 figure code read 参考波 and 物波); the laser mirror is 反射镜 (the concept statement read 两面镜子); “the deceleration of electrons” reads 电子减速时 with the span on 减速 (a scenario of 30.4); the peak of a spectrum is 峰 (30.4 and the 30.6 alt text read 峰值); the bare noun state is 状态, with 态 kept inside compounds and level labels (30.3, 30.5 and 30.8 read 态); laser pointer is 激光指示器 (30.5 read 激光笔); shell is 壳层 and subshell 支壳层 (provisional; 次壳层 and 亚壳层 compete) and the spin projection quantum number 自旋磁量子数. The section titles of 30.7 and 30.8 were already 光谱中的规律揭示更多的量子化 and 量子数及其规则. The derivation source of 30.6 now reads 《大学物理（第2版）》，30.6 物质的波动性导致量子化 as 30.3 does; the PhET names are in “” with the English in parentheses; the credits stay as printed in English after （来源：…） (the one joined with 、 and 和 in 30.5 now reads as printed, and Unknown Author stays English); unit symbols in axis labels are half-width (E (eV), r (nm), s (nm)); the part labels of prompts, solutions and notes are full-width （a）（b）, as are the choice keys; the apparatus words of the notes are 判分选择题, 书后答案, 建议思路, 布鲁姆层级, 习题卡片 and “理解检测”框, AP 题 and 概念标签; the spacing of α 粒子 and π 介子 was repaired. The `\text{；因此，}` of 30.9 keeps full-width punctuation inside the math, as chapters 15 and 16 do. The page translators had listed 40 scenarios (30.2: 5, 30.3: 9, 30.4: 4, 30.5: 17, 30.6: 2, 30.7: 2, 30.9: 1); the merge dropped the plain choices (cited titles in “”, the \text word 或, the article-less data-ref spans and the span orders of 30.3, 30.4 and 30.5, the hyphenated-modifier remark of 30.3, the credit of 30.5, the `answer.parts[].part` rows of 30.3 and 30.5, and the 30.9 branch that leaves no difference in code), merged the two above/below reorders of 30.2, and added the deceleration row; the parity checker's `figures.js: code` lines (30.2 five, 30.5 nine, 30.6 two, 30.7 one) are all recorded.

## Chapter 31 sections

Merge of 2026-10-23. Decisions taken for the chapter (owner-directed; the glossary wins over page drift unless listed here): the title of 31.2 is 辐射的探测与探测器; the range of radiation is 辐射的射程 as a concept name; parent and daughter nuclei are 母核 and 子核 (the sense rows parent (nucleus) and daughter); electron family number is 电子轻子数 (the literal 电子族数 is noted and not used); radioactive dating is 放射性鉴年法 (termonline 物理学名词), carbon-14 dating 碳-14 鉴年法 and the bare dating 鉴年, so 31.5 no longer reads 年代测定; binding energy per nucleon is 比结合能 (unit `\text{MeV/核子}`); the gravitational potential energy of the assembled pieces of the Earth is 引力势能 (a sense row); the Geiger tube is 盖革管; a track is 径迹, a pulse of current 脉冲, the cylinder and the wire of the tube 圆筒 and 导线 (sense rows); the scanning tunneling microscope is 扫描隧穿显微镜 and the marble’s tunnel 隧穿通道; the rim of the bowl is 碗沿 and the microscope’s probe 探针; the person Becquerel is 贝克勒尔 and the unit becquerel 贝可勒尔 (31.1 read 贝可勒尔 for the person); a photographic plate is 照相底片 (the row read 感光板); the rays of radioactivity are 射线; radioactive before a noun is 放射性; the unified atomic mass unit is written 原子质量单位 because termonline lists no 统一原子质量单位 (the row, the concept atomic-mass-unit and 31.3 and 31.6 follow); substructure is 子结构 (the intro and the title of 31.3); the phosphor of Rutherford’s screen is 磷光体, the residual nucleus 剩余核, shell closure 壳层封闭, the body burden 体内放射性负荷 and the gas lantern mantle 汽灯纱罩, all as termonline or the glossary has them; the credit of Figure 31.29 is kept in English as printed; cited titles are in “”; part labels and figure citations in prose are full-width （a）（b）; the sentence-final stop inside `\text{中微子。}` is full-width. The pending rows of the seven pages are folded into the glossary (81 added, 29 changed; the 14 concepts that had a row without a concept link got the link), the eight pages were read against the glossary targets, and the concept rows atomic-mass-unit, radioactive-dating and nuclear-shell, the form eq-becquerel, the title of 31.3 in chapter.json and the variables 31.1 B_mag and 31.7 d (now `"redefines": true`, reworded to stand alone) were set with ost.

## Chapter 32 sections

Merge of 2026-10-23. Decisions taken for the chapter: the glossary rows won over the page translators where the source was already a row (spoilage 变质 for 腐败, irradiation plant 辐照装置 for 辐照厂, fission bomb 原子弹 for 裂变弹, tactical and strategic nuclear weapons 战术核武器 and 战略核武器 for the shortened 战术武器 and 战略武器, atmospheric tests 大气层核试验, D-T pellet and D-T target 氘氚靶丸 and 氘氚靶 for the letters D-T, hypothyroidism 甲状腺功能减退症, long-term effects 长期效应, carcinogenic 致癌的, PhET simulation PhET 模拟 for 仿真 and 互动程序); loss of coolant accident is the one reverse case: the page’s 冷却剂丧失事故 is the termonline form (电力名词, the 失水事故 listed is a maritime term), so the glossary row is changed to it; the products of a reaction are 产物 throughout (生成物 was changed), medium-mass nuclei are 中等质量核 in 32.5 and 32.6, and the squash court is 壁球馆 (termonline 建筑学名词). Sense rows were added where the page’s sense differs: contrast (medical imaging) 对比度, reaction (nuclear) 反应, toroidal coil (tokamak) 环向场线圈, confinement (fusion) 约束, core (reactor) 堆芯, beam (radiation) 射束 (so Crossfire beams is 交叉射束), hair cells (hair growth) 毛发细胞 and source (radioactive) 放射源; rad (unit) 拉德 and cell (biological) 细胞 were already rows. Attractive nuclear force is 吸引性核力 in prose and the figure label stays 核引力 with its span; kT and MT are printed as the prose prints them, in the figure strings of 32.7 too (the glossary rows that wrote kt and Mt are changed); Styrofoam stays English in the figures as in the text; 腐败 was changed back to 变质 in the text, the lead and the concept row of 32.4; the added 事故 of the first paragraph of 32.6 stays and is recorded. The section notes use one wording for the book’s apparatus (书后答案, 建议思路, 容差, PhET 模拟). The 23 rows below are the forced scenarios; the page translators’ plain choices (the span on one part of a compound, the cited section titles, the stray ? of the 32.5 cq3 prompt, the tagline in the alt of the 32.3 photo) are not recorded.
