## For the owner

1. **`answer.unit` and `answer.parts[].part` stay English.** translate.md 5 lists both as kept byte for byte, so 1.2 (`length in feet`, `length in inches`, `width in feet`, `width in inches`) and 1.3 (`figures`, `pounds`, `beats`, `beats/min`, `(a) lowest`, `heart rate`, `volume`, `area`, `uncertainty`) keep their English words, although a reader may see them beside the answer box. The owner decides whether these become translatable fields; if they do, translate.md 5 and the content check change first, then these strings.
2. **Figure eyebrows and citations.** The eyebrows `Figure N.M` and `Sim` stay English in every section until the app localises them (translate.md 4.6), and the prose cites figures as 图 N.M, which stays plain text: the app's figure-link pattern matches only `Figure N.M` and does not yet read 图 N.M. Recorded once for the book, not per citation.
3. **Glossary decisions of the 2026-10-22 merge** that the owner or termonline should confirm: the photo-credit label 来源 (1.2's 图片来源 changed), heat 热量 everywhere (1.2's subject list changed from 热), speed of light 光速 as the span of the short form beside 真空中的光速, 估读 for reading a scale's last digit beside 估算 (1.3 changed), 扫描隧穿显微镜 and 放射性年代测定. Each row's `note` gives the reason; termonline.cn could not be reached, so each still carries `confirm`.
4. **Glossary decisions of the chapter 2 merge (2026-10-22)** to confirm: 航天员 for astronaut (宇航员 is the everyday word), 起始位置 (starting position) as its own row beside 初位置, 末速率 for final speed, 具有加速度 for "is accelerating" when only the direction changes, 纵截距（*y* 截距） for y-intercept, 误区提醒 and 居家实验 for the apparatus labels, 时刻 for a point in time. In 2.4 the slider subscript `\text{top}` is written `\text{最大}` (the source word is top, not max, so it is not a `\text{max}` symbol). Every new row carries `confirm` until termonline is reachable.
5. **gravity: 重力 or 引力 (judgement, 2026-10-22).** Source "gravity" became 重力 where it is the Earth's (or the Moon's) pull on things near the surface: 2.7 (引力与自由落体运动 heading, the free-fall prose, the hammer-and-feather caption, the sim line) and 3.4 ("gravity is weaker there" on the Moon). It stays 引力 for "gravitational field" (1.1, the ch01 concept rows), "Newton's theory of gravity" (universal gravitation), "from lightning to gravity" (1.1, gravity as a fundamental interaction) and gravitational lensing (1.2).
6. **Glossary decisions of the chapter 4 merge (2026-10-22)** to confirm. Weight and gravity: weight (the force w = mg) is 重力 everywhere, its explanans is 引力, the Earth's pull on a body standing alone is 重力, the pull between bodies and the fundamental force are 引力 (the scenario row under ch04/4.3); the headings 质量与重力, 斜面上的重力 and 把重力分解为分量 and the concept names mass-versus-weight and weight-components-on-incline follow; the everyday weight of mass (重量, 减重) and a load (重物) are rows of their own. Graph axes are 横轴/纵轴 (rows horizontal axis (of a graph), vertical axis (of a graph)) and the spatial axes of a scene stay 水平轴/竖直轴. The noun push as a force is 推力 throughout chapter 4 (row push (force); decided at the 2026-10-22 ch04 review), 推 only the verb; electromagnetism as the interaction is 电磁相互作用 (4.8), 电磁学 the discipline. Where a page differed from an existing row the row won: 外加合力 (not 总外加力), 内部结构 (not 亚结构), 滚动摩擦 (not 滚动摩擦力), and the three physicists' names 罗杰·彭罗斯, 赖因哈德·根策尔, 安德烈娅·盖兹 (not the English). The 20<sup></sup> 世纪 form for "20th century" is accepted (ch04/intro). Every new row carries `confirm` until termonline is reachable.
7. **Glossary decisions of the chapter 5 merge (2026-10-22)** to confirm. Friction: the force is 滑动摩擦力 and 静摩擦力, the magnitudes 滑动摩擦力的大小 and 静摩擦力和滑动摩擦力的大小, while the coefficients keep the GB 3102 names 动摩擦因数 and 静摩擦因数 (note on the row coefficient of kinetic friction). Drag: the coefficient is 阻力系数 (the headings 阻力系数的值 and 阻力系数与物体的形状) and the force is 阻力 (曳力 was the form until 2026-10-22). Pencil lead is 铅笔的“铅” (the pun survives in Chinese), the hip prosthesis cup is 髋臼杯, high spots and peaks of a rough surface are both 凸起处, bodysuits worn by bicyclists, swimmers and runners are 全身紧身服 beside 紧身泳衣 for swimwear, the sled-dog command mush is 冲啊, and the label of a wall in Figure 5.17 is 墙. Each row's `note` gives the reason; termonline.cn could not be reached, so the technical rows carry `confirm`.
8. **Glossary decisions of the chapter 6 merge (2026-10-22)** to confirm. Gravity: the pull between bodies and the fundamental force is 引力 throughout chapter 6 (6.3's first paragraph, 地球对月球的引力; 6.5's opening, Newton, Moon, tide, black-hole and Cavendish passages, its lead, cq1, cq2 and problem 5; the two notes of 6.4 that say where gravity is introduced; the concept and heading 引力与月球的轨道 and 引力作为月球的向心力; the PhET name 引力与轨道（Gravity and Orbits）). 重力 stays for weight, for the Earth's pull on a body at its surface (the feet, the apple, the blood column, the plants), for the named quantity 重力加速度 wherever the source says “the acceleration due to gravity” (also at altitude and at the Moon's distance, as the row says) and for 零重力 and 微重力. Atmospheric pressure is 气压 (row pressure (atmospheric), authority cnterm-atmos, added to `authorities`), written 低气压 and 高气压 with the pressure span on 气压; 低压 stays for the low of a weather system (the LOW label), and 气块, 气旋 and the other weather rows now cite cnterm-atmos (the two 7.7 weather rows, global warming and lightning bolt, still cite termonline for the ch07 pass to settle). Static friction “a responsive force” is 响应式的力 as in 5.1 (6.3's 随需而变的力 changed), separation of two bodies or points is 间距 (6.2's 间隔 changed), and “the same two velocities, laid tail to tail” is 矢尾对矢尾 (it read 首尾相接, which is head to tail). Cited chapter and section titles take “” (《》 in 6.4's text and the 6.1 note changed; bare titles in the chapter intro and in the 6.4 and 6.6 notes quoted). rev/min and rpm stay as the source prints them and 转每分 stands only where the source writes the words (6.1 problem 7 changed). **太阴月** for the 27.3-day “lunar month” of 6.5 problem 5(b) is the book's word and stays; astronomy calls 27.3 d the 恒星月 (sidereal month) and 太阴月 the 29.5-day synodic month, so the owner decides whether the problem reads 恒星月. Answer-part labels and units that stay English as kept fields (the question of item 1): 6.1 p3 `unit` rotations and p7 `in rev/min`; the `part` labels of 6.2 p5, p7, p9, p11, p12 and p13 and of 6.5 p3, p7 and p10. Variants not added as rows because an existing row covers them: Problems and Exercises (row Problems & Exercises 习题), Earth radius (row radius of Earth 地球半径), and the repeated Unreasonable Results and kinetic energy. Every new row carries `confirm` until termonline is reachable.
9. **Glossary decisions of the chapter 7 merge (2026-10-22)** to confirm. Where the labels agent's concept statements and the existing rows disagreed, the existing row won and the statement was changed: turbine 涡轮机 (not 汽轮机, though the book's turbine is a steam turbine), coal-fired power plant 燃煤电厂 (not 燃煤发电厂), hydroelectric power and the noun hydroelectric 水电 (not 水力发电; the row hydroelectric read 水力发电的 and 7.9's prose had 水力发电), solar cell 太阳电池, electric clock 电钟; the owner may prefer the three Chinese forms the statements first had. Energy in the resource, consumption or economic sense is 能源 (7.6 p4 and its note, 7.9's “所使用的能源”, the statements of fossil-fuels and energy-degradation, the 7.9 variables E and P; 节能 for energy conservation in energy-degradation); the physical quantity stays 能量. The Earth's pull on a body is 重力 even where the source says gravitational force (7.3's work against the gravitational force and “by or against” sentences, the cuckoo-clock weight sentence, 7.6 cq on the dam water); 引力 stays for gravitation between bodies, the gravitational field, and 7.1's “gravitational force on it always points at the center” for a satellite in orbit (left, as the Moon in chapter 6). Marble: the row marble is now the ball 弹珠 (old 大理石), and the stone, which does occur in 5.3's table of materials, has its own row marble (stone) 大理石. Track: track (rail) 轨道, track (running) 跑道, track alone 航迹; cart (lab) 小车 also covers the roller-coaster cart of 7.2 (it read 手推车); wind resistance is 空气阻力 (7.7 p13 read 风阻); bar (an energy bar) is 柱条 in 7.2–7.5 (7.2 read 柱形), the stacked bar of 7.6 is 叠柱; graded choice is 判分选择题 and the suggested approach 建议思路 in every note; the year slider and axis of 7.9 read 年份; solar collection farms and the solar cooking program follow the rows 太阳能收集场 and 太阳灶推广计划. Subscripts that are words are translated (7.8's \text{sleep}, \text{class}, \text{cycle} are 睡眠, 上课, 骑车; F_start and F_end are 初 and 末); those that are symbols stay (\text{ave}, \text{net}, \text{max}, \text{min}, \text{tot}, \text{Eff}). “kinesthetic energy” in 7.2's ct1 solution is the book's misprint for kinetic energy, kept as 动觉能量; the owner may prefer 动能. The source has no “Connections” heading in chapter 7, so no row was made for it. The 29 ost check warnings left are all in ch08/chapter.json (redefined variable meanings), not in chapter 7. Every new row carries `confirm` until termonline is reachable.
10. **Glossary decisions of the chapter 8 merge (2026-10-22)** to confirm. Carts: the lab and physics carts of chapter 8 are 小车 everywhere (the rows cart A, cart B, cart 1, m₁, cart 2, m₂, the first cart, the second cart, the Example 8.6 title, the object-1 and object-2 labels of 8.5 and the compressed spring cart 1 carries changed from 手推车, which stays for the hand cart of ch04); 气垫小车 for air car and 气垫导轨 for air track. Momentum: 动量 for the physics term, 线动量 where the source says “linear momentum”, 运动量 for the historical “quantity of motion”, and 势头（momentum） for the everyday sense in the chapter opening (the English kept, translate.md 4.2). The conservation principle follows the source word (动量守恒原理 for “principle”, 动量守恒 otherwise). Senses kept apart by new rows: impact (strike) 撞击 beside the landing 着地, catapult (aircraft carrier) 弹射器 beside the siege 投石机, crater (lunar) 环形山 beside the volcano’s 火山口, bumper (pool table) 库边 beside the car’s 保险杠, stroke (tennis) 击球动作 beside the swimming 划水动作, spin (of a particle) 自旋 and spin (of a ball) 旋转, billiards 台球 with pool 桌球. Gravity: 重力 for the pull on a body near the Earth (the thrown ball of 8.2, the rocket against its weight and “air resistance and gravitational force” in 8.7), 引力 only between bodies (“escape Earth’s gravity”, the cited title “匀速圆周运动与引力”). “Sound” as the audible thing is 声音 (8.4 changed from 声; row sound). Solid rocket boosters and the source’s “solid fuel boosters” are both 固体火箭助推器 (8.7 changed from 固体燃料助推器). Racquet strings are 拍线 (8.5 changed from 拍弦), the belly flop is 肚皮拍水 (8.3 changed from 腹部拍水), and the notes of 8.1, 8.3 and the AP items follow chapter 7’s wording (书后答案, 建议思路, AP 题). The book’s tag Professional Application is 专业应用 and the pending 8.5 problem 7 keeps launcher 发射器 beside the text’s launch vehicle 运载火箭 (the same rocket; the owner may unify). The credit “NASA” of 8.7 stays as printed, as in the eight earlier credits, although the row NASA is 美国国家航空航天局. Each new technical row carries `confirm` until termonline is reachable; a few rows (open system 开放系统, ultrasound 超声, target 靶, impact parameter 碰撞参数, helium nucleus 氦原子核, gold 金) cite 物理学名词 or 化学名词 from the page translators’ memory and were not looked up.
11. **Glossary decisions of the chapter 9 merge (2026-10-22)** to confirm. Net force: in prose and labels net F is 合力 and net external force 合外力, net torque 合力矩 and net external torque 合外力矩; inside an equation the word stays `\text{net}` (the page translators had `\text{合力}` and `\text{合力矩}` in 9.1, 9.2, 9.4, 9.5 and 9.6, all changed back), so the 9.2 sentence “where net means total” reads 其中 net（合）的意思是总的 and is a scenario row. cw and ccw are likewise abbreviations that stay in a subscript (`\tau_\text{cw}` in 9.2 problem 5 had been written 顺时针). Equilibrium is 静态平衡 and 动态平衡 everywhere: the rows Static equilibrium and dynamic equilibrium, Problem-Solving Strategy: Static Equilibrium Situations and Solving a static equilibrium problem and the concept name static-equilibrium-strategy (now 求解静态平衡问题) read 静平衡, and the notes of the rows static equilibrium and dynamic equilibrium were garbled (“静态平衡 is the competing school form, not used”) and are rewritten; 静平衡 and 动平衡 name rotor balancing. 枢轴 is gone from the glossary: nine chapter 10 rows still had it (the nail, the pivot; the torque about the pivot; and the others in `change`) and now read 支点. Headings the glossary fixes, where a page differed and the page was changed: 平衡的三种类型 (9.3, it read 三种平衡), 作为杠杆的肌肉、骨骼与关节, 肌肉为什么附着在靠近关节处 and 真实系统中的复杂情形 (9.6, with “真实系统” in that section’s lead and first sentence). The five rows Example 9.1 to 9.5 carried the prefix 例题 N： that the pages print as 例题 N · , so the prefix is dropped from the rows as in chapter 8.
12. **Chapter 9 terms where one English word has two Chinese forms by sense** (each a row, none a divergence). Load is 载荷 for the force on a lever or machine (the wheelbarrow, the biceps and its load, 9.6 now 载荷 throughout; it read 负荷, and 9.3 had 荷载), 重物 for an object carried, hung or balanced (the load on the head, the load carried on the back or in front of the body, the weight held near the cg). The owner may want 载荷 for the carried object too. Handle is 手柄 for a lever or tool (row handle (of a lever or tool); the nail puller, the wrench, the shovel), 把手 for the handle of a wheel (existing row) and 握把 for the grip of the hockey stick (row grip). Attachment is 连接处 for the cables of a pulley system and 附着处 for the muscle’s (it read 附着点 in one sentence of 9.6). Screw is 螺钉 for the fastener and 螺旋 for the simple machine; barrow is 手推车 as the cart of chapter 4, beside 独轮手推车 for the wheelbarrow; lift is 抬 as the act of raising and 升力 for the aerodynamic force; ramp is 坡道 in the sentence on the pyramids, 斜面 for the incline of the figure, 匝道 on the freeway; bar is 条形 for the bars of a figure that compare quantities (9.4 read 条), 柱条 for the energy bars of chapter 7 and 叠柱 for its stacked bar; “mechanical system” in the caption of Figure 9.25 is 力学系统 (机械系统 is the other option); “linear motion” is 直线运动.
13. **Critical point (9.3).** 临界点 is the term for the point where the cg leaves the base of support (物理学名词), and the source tags it with the concept id critical-point, which is the phase-diagram concept of 13.5 (name “Critical point” in the book). The row critical point carries that concept id so that chapter 13 keeps the same name; the two senses are not the same idea, which the owner may want to separate in the source tags.
14. **Other terms of chapter 9** that cite 物理学名词 or 人体解剖学名词 from the translators’ memory and were not looked up: critical point, machine 机械, resting length 静息长度, neck vertebrae 颈椎, neck 颈, ankle 踝, kneecap (the patella) 髌骨（膝盖骨）, knife edge 刀口, anchorage 锚碇 (锚锭 is a variant), central nervous system 中枢神经系统, rain gutter 檐沟. The prompt of 9.6 problem 3 writes 髌骨（膝盖骨）, a scenario row, since the glossary maps kneecap and patella to the same 髌骨. Walker 助行器, tail (animal) 尾巴, free end 自由端, lawn mower 割草机 and the football tackle 擒抱 already had rows and are not repeated.
15. **Cited title in 9.6.** The text and the notes cite the section “Collisions of Extended Bodies in Two Dimensions” of chapter 10; the translator’s 二维扩展物体的碰撞 was replaced by the glossary’s “有限大小物体的二维碰撞” (row Collisions of Extended Bodies in Two Dimensions, 10.6), in “” as plain text. If chapter 10 titles the section differently, this changes with it.
16. **weight words kept.** 9.1’s “exactly as much as he weighs” stays 他的重量 (the everyday weighing, in the sim-person caption and its headline); 9.5’s “the barrow itself is not weightless” now reads 手推车本身也有重力 (it read 重量, a weight of X). The explanans of weight in the variable w of 9.1 keeps “即作用在它上面的引力”.
17. **Source slips noticed in chapter 9.** The alt text of the figure of 9.3 problem 9 says “the centers of the gravity of the two legs” (sic, for the center of gravity of each leg), and the text of 9.5 says “Inclined lanes or ramps” for inclined planes. Both are kept as far as Chinese can carry them (the leg centers of gravity as printed in sense, 斜坡 for the lanes). The source also tags the 9.3 sentence on the point where the cg leaves the base of support with the phase-diagram concept critical-point (item 13).
18. **Glossary decisions of the chapter 10 merge (2026-10-22)** to confirm. Rotational inertia and moment of inertia are one term, 转动惯量 (the book uses them as synonyms): the 10.3 section title 转动动力学：转动惯量, the short title, the Figure 10.12 caption 几种转动惯量 and every sentence of 10.3 (the page translators had 转动惯性 in four places, now changed); only the h2 “Rotational Inertia and Moment of Inertia” reads 转动惯性与转动惯量, since both English words are printed there (the glossary row of that heading changed to match). Pivot is 支点 everywhere (10.7's 枢轴 in the text and the sim hover names changed). Axle is 转轴 for the axis a body spins about, the gyroscope's and the 10.7 bicycle wheel's included (the row “L, the angular momentum of the wheel, along its axle” changed 车轴 → 转轴), and 车轴 only for the axle of a vehicle in the 10.3 bicycle sim. Linear kinetic energy is 平动动能 and linear motion 直线运动; the retarding torque of Earth's rotation is 阻碍力矩 (the page read 减速力矩; the pending row follows the concept statement). Net angular momentum is 总角动量, altitude of an aircraft 高度, air mass 气团, hard disk 硬盘, motorcycle rider 骑手. Gravitational energy of the helicopter is 重力势能 (a sense row beside 引力能) and of a gas cloud 引力势能. The helicopter's “weight limitations” stays 重量的限制 (the everyday weight). Bars: 条形 for the bars of a figure that compare quantities and 柱条 for energy bars, as the existing rows bar (chart) and bar (energy bar) decide.
19. **Net in math, chapters 9 and 10 differ.** Chapter 9's merge kept `\text{net}` inside equations (item 11); chapter 10 follows the page-level decision `\text{合力矩}\;\tau`, `\text{合力}\;F` and `\text{合外力的功}\;W` (10.5 and 10.6 had `\text{net}` and were changed; so were six form rows and the statement of torque-changes-angular-momentum in the chapter's rows). Subscripts (`_{\text{net}}`, `_{\text{ball}}`, `_{\text{nail}}`, `_{\text{disk}}`) stay English. The owner may want one rule for both chapters. The glossary rows “Example N.M: …” keep the colon as lookup keys while every page prints `例题 N.M · 标题`.

The translation reads 收缩 (the book's word cannot be kept in Chinese), so the misprint is not carried over; keep or change to 接触.

23. **Chapter 11 term decisions (owner-directed at the merge).** Atmospheric pressure is 大气压 everywhere, with 标准大气压 for standard atmospheric pressure and 气压计 for the barometer. Adhesion is 附着, adhesive force 附着力, cohesion 内聚, cohesive force 内聚力. A figurative “ruler” of pressures is 标尺 (the physical ruler stays 直尺), conversion factor is 换算因子, cork as a material is 软木 and the stopper 软木塞, the surface of a liquid in the 11.7 sims is 液面, the “Floating Woman” is 漂浮的女士, psi is read as the glossary says and pounds per square inch is 磅每平方英寸. Weight is 重力 for the force, 体重 for a person in 11.9, 重量 for everyday weighing and 称重 for the act. Confirm or reverse; each is a glossary row first and then a change everywhere the term stands.
24. **Adhesion 附着 against termonline and ch05.** The glossary row adhesion (5.1) carried termonline's 黏附 (物理学名词, surface physics, confirmed 2026-10-22) and was changed to 附着 on the owner decision. `ch05/5.1` (text.html, section.json and figures.js, 7 places) still reads 黏附 for the friction sense, so chapters 5 and 11 now differ until one of them is changed; the glossary row 黏附力 (adhesive forces) was a plural that no page used and now agrees with 附着力. Likewise the row `pressure (atmospheric)` in 6.4 still reads 气压 and was left alone; if 大气压 is meant for it too, ch06 needs the same pass.

The only glossary row that read 大卡 for a lower-case calorie was the row `Calorie` (section 2.2; the lookup is case-insensitive), so it is changed to 卡, and a sense row `Calorie (food Calorie, capital C)` 大卡 is added because ch02/2.2 (“250 Calories”) and ch15/15.1 print the capital C and read 大卡. 14.1’s sentence “Food calories (given the notation Cal, and sometimes called “big calorie”)” reads 食物卡路里（记作 Cal，即“大卡”） so it is not circular (“sometimes called” becomes 即; a forced scenario; the row Food calories (the sentence that defines Cal) is the book’s own wording, `confirm: owner`). 48. **Other term decisions of chapter 14 (owner-directed).** Temperature difference is 温差 everywhere (14.2, 14.3, 14.4, 14.6, the concept rows and the variable meanings read 温度差; 14.1’s 温度的差异 for “the difference in temperatures” stays; the rows Heat is energy transferred because of a temperature difference, A small temperature difference across a good conductor, Finding the temperature difference from a heat flow, the temperature difference across a pocket and Heat pumps and temperature difference follow). The last row names the concept of ch15/15.5, whose page, concept name and statements still read 温度差 for the chapter 15 merge. Cork the material is 软木 (Table 14.3 and the sim option; the stopper stays 软木塞). Vapor in general is 蒸气 and water vapor 水蒸气 (three bare “vapor” of the 14.3 solutions read 水蒸气 and now read 蒸气). Forced convection is 强制对流, a new row (14.6 read 强迫对流; the concept natural-convection already said 强制对流). “Macroscopic movement of mass” is 物质的宏观运动 (14.6 summary, the span on mass wrapping 物质): no glossary row or concept row carried 质量的宏观运动, so there was nothing to change but the summary and a new row; the row macroscopic movement 宏观运动 (the movement of a fluid in 14.4) is unchanged and 14.4 read 宏观移动. A person at rest is 休息时 (14.7 read 静止的人; its “normal resting rate” now reads 休息时 … 的正常速率); the ch07 rows person at rest 静止状态的人 and Power consumed at rest 静息时 and the ch12 row at rest (circulation) 静息 are other senses and stay, but the owner may want one word. A circus rider is 骑手, a beam of radiation 辐射束, a net rate or net transfer 净 (the row net 合力 is the force column), a block of material 物块 (the row block (object) already said so), the Earth’s surface 地表 (the row Earth’s surface changed from 地球表面; 14.4 and 14.7 read 地球表面 and 地球的表面温度) and a rate of transfer 速率 (seven places read 速度; “as fast as it can evaporate” in a 14.3 solution stays 速度). The thermal insulator stays 隔热体 for this chapter (“insulators of electricity” in 14.5 is 绝缘体; ch18 gives the electrical insulator its row). Credits are as printed: 14.7’s “（来源：美国陆军）” is “（来源：U.S. Army）”; the row U.S. Army is for prose. Cited titles are in “”: 14.7 had 《电磁波》, 《量子物理学引论》 and two titles in its notes in 《》, and only this book’s own title takes 《》. Shock wave is 冲击波 (the row changed from 激波; the termonline note had confirmed 激波 as the 规范名 of 力学名词, 冲击波 being the variant; 物理学名词 lists both; 17.7 read 激波 once). Pitch of a sound is 音调 (new sense row pitch (of a sound) with the concept pitch; the bare row pitch now reads 音调 and the new row pitch (of a vehicle) 俯仰 keeps the rotation of ch10 10.7, whose figures are unchanged). The book's “sound level” is its shorthand for sound intensity level (row sound level changed from 声级 to 声强级; 声学名词 声级 is a weighted level); sound intensity 声强, decibel 分贝. Hearing is 听觉 for the perception and 听力 for the ability (hearing loss 听力损失, hearing test 听力测试, normal hearing 听力正常), with the new sense rows hearing protection 听力防护 and hearing damage 听力损伤; hearing (ability) 听力 stood already from the ch16 merge. The note of the row first harmonic is corrected: the fundamental is the first harmonic and harmonics are numbered from it. Tone is 音质 in Table 17.4, 纯音 for a pure tone and 声音 for “a 440 Hz tone” in the figure strings of 17.2 (new sense row tone (a sound of one frequency)). Aircraft and plane are 飞机 in this chapter (sense rows aircraft (airplane) and plane (aircraft) beside aircraft 航空器), an ultrasound probe is 探头 and a wave used as a probe 探针 (two sense rows beside probe 探针), the mouse of 17.4 problem 3 is 老鼠 (row mouse (everyday), ch15), and the title of 17.1 is 声音 (book row Sound).

The magnetic force is 磁力 (no page read 磁场力). A cosmic ray is 宇宙线 (22.5 read 宇宙射线 throughout; 宇宙射线 is the everyday form). The compass is 指南针 and its needle 指南针的指针: the glossary row compass needle changes from 罗盘指针, and **ch04/4.8 still reads 罗盘指针** and needs the same change at its next pass (the row compass direction 罗盘方向 of 3.2 is another row and was left). rms is 方均根 (22.10 read 均方根). The supersonic aircraft is 超声速飞机 (22.4 read 超音速飞机, 22.6 超声速航空器). The counter-torque of a meter is 反向力矩 (22.8 read 反力矩; the row counter-torque changes), and a spiral path is 螺旋路径 (the row spiral paths changes from 螺旋轨迹 so that “circular or spiral path” reads as a pair with 圆形路径). The force per unit length is 单位长度上的力 and the turns per unit length 单位长度上的匝数 (the concept force-per-unit-length-on-a-wire is named 导线单位长度上的力; its glossary row changes). A current loop is 载流线圈 (the book’s “circular current loop” and “closed-current loop” of 22.3 and 22.2 read 圆形电流环 and 闭合电流环路). The Biot-Savart law keeps its hyphen (毕奥-萨伐尔定律). The return spring of a meter is 回复弹簧 (termonline: 游丝 and 回复弹簧 both to be checked). In prose magnetism is 磁性 (new row magnetism (property)); 磁学 stays for the chapter title and the cited section titles 磁学导论 and 磁学的更多应用.

220. **termonline.** The site was reachable (the lookups ran 2026-10-23). Of the 101 new rows, 25 are the book’s own labels and hover names (`confirm: owner`); 17 are confirmed with their entry URL, 14 are listed under another form or sense and kept with the reason in the note, 45 are not listed and are taken from the named authority; none stays provisional. The 336 harvest rows of the intro and 32.x still marked `confirm: termonline` were also looked up (`verify-ch32.json` in the scratchpad): 103 agree, 29 are listed under another form, 191 are not listed, 13 are composite labels with no lookup. Besides the rows named in items 211 and 212, the differing ones that touch the pages are exposure (照射; termonline 照射量), fallout (放射性沉降物; 沉降物, 散落物), fission fuel and fusion fuel (裂变核燃料, 聚变核燃料), fusion power plant (聚变电厂), containment (包容, 安全壳) and enriched uranium; none was changed.
221. **Wording of the section notes.** The notes and exercise_notes of the seven sections now use the apparatus words of the glossary in one form: 书后答案 (for 本书自己的答案表, 本书自带的答案, 书中自带的答案 and 答案表), 建议思路 (for 建议解法), 容差 in the sentence “数值答案接受各题卡片给出的容差，未给出时为 2%”, PhET 模拟 (for 仿真 and 互动程序), the part label （c）问 in full width.
